1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/sid d+hi e ka pI ra ma hA tan tra rA dzA nA ma/bod skad du/
1b2(rgyud): dpa' bo gcig bu grub pa zhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po//bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gzhon nur 
1b3(rgyud): gyur pa la phyag 'tshal lo//dpa' bo gcig grub dpa' bo'i gtso//rab mchog smra ba rnams kyi mchog/
1b4(rgyud): 'gro ba'i bla ma 'jam rdo rjes//'jig rten don du rgyud rab gsungs//lha nyid gsang sngags tshul gyis na//
1b5(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad stsol mdzad pa//sngags pas de nyid bsgoms na ni//da grub na ni grub 'gyur te//
1b6(rgyud): sngags pa lus ni rnam dag dang //gang tshe dngos grub phra mo 'grub//snod du gyur pa'i lus la ni//dngos 
1b7(rgyud): grub chen po rab tu skye//rim pa 'di yis 'di la ni//sa la sogs pa 'thob pa skye//sngags pas yid 
1b8(rgyud): bzhin nor bu ltar//rtag tu sems can don byed do//de ltar mkhyen nas thugs brtses non//rdo rje 'chang sogs 
2a1(rgyud): @#//kyis zhus tshe//grub pa'i gsang sngags bsdus pa ni//'gro ba'i don du gsungs pa yin//oM kA lu me   lu kA lu me lu staM b+ha ya s+thi lA ba r+NaM tu ShA ri tsa lu ts+tsha i lu ts+tsha i swAhA//gsang sngags kyi rgyal po 
2a2(rgyud): 'di ni legs par bzlas na ser ba gcod par byed do//dran pa nyid dang thal ba la mngon par bzlas pas ser ba 'bab pa 'am kha ba 'bab pa gcod par 'gyur zhing//gang du 'dod pa der 'gro'o//de bzhin du klog dang 
2a3(rgyud): rlung dang rdo rje dang thog 'bab pa la sogs pa med par byed do//oM ga ru Da ha~M sa ha~M he /tsa la tsa la swA hA /sngags kyi rgyal po 'di dran pa nyid na ser ba 'bab pa zlog par byed do//rdo rgyus kyis rnga paT+Ta ha la bris shing /rnga 
2a4(rgyud): lcag la mngon par bzlas pa'i rnga ba T+Ta ha'i sgras/ser pa 'bab pa zlog par byed de//de bzhin du dung la sogs pa'i sgras kyang bya'o//oM he he tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/d+hA ra ya d+hA ra ya/ni ru na b+ha ni ru na d+ha/che ge mo u r+N+Na ma Ni swAhA/gsang sngags kyi 
2a5(rgyud): rgyal po 'di sngon du bsnyen pa'i tshogs 'bum phrag gcig yongs su bzlas na 'grub par 'gyur ro//glog dang rlung dang rdo rje dang thogs 'bab pa'am kha ba la sogs pa dran pa tsam nyid kyis zlog par byed do//thal ba dang 
2a6(rgyud): yungs kar la mngon par bzlas na/char ches pa gcod pa dang byi ba dang sbrang ma la sogs pa'i mchu 'ching bar byed do//skyed mos tshal dang zhing la bya dang 'bu srin dang byi ba rnams kyis nye bar 'tshe bzlog par byed do//dur 
2a7(rgyud): khrod kyi ras la ming dang bcas pa'i sngags bris la spra tshil gyis btums te/dur khrod kyi bum par bcug nas kha bcad de/de nyid du sbas pas mngal rengs par 'gyur ro//phyung nas 'o ma dang chus bkrus te chu la bor bas 
2a8(rgyud): zhi bar 'gyur ro//yung ba 'am bab blas gro ba 'am gos sam lo ma la mngon par bris te/spra tshil gyi nang du btums pa'i sngags 'di grog mkhar gyi sa las 'jigs byed kyi gzugs su byas pa de'i snying khar gcub ste 
2b1(rgyud): de'i kha thal bas bkang nas sbas na/rgol ba dang phyir rgol ba'i kha rengs par byed do//rtsod sbyong byed pa na gzugs de nyid kyi lce la lan bdun mngon par bsngags pa'i tsher ma bdun gyis btsugs na kha 'ching bar byed par 
2b2(rgyud): 'gyur ro//rdo rgyus kyis kham por la bris te thal bas bkang la/kha sbyar te sbas na mi snyan pa brjod pa kha rengs par byed do/rdza mkhan gyi lag pa phyis pa'i 'jim pa las/lug gi gzugs su byas pa'i snying khar sbra tshil 
2b3(rgyud): gyis btums pa'i sngags bcug na khros pa'i kha rengs par byed do//ske 'am dpung pa la sngags btab na g.yul dogs kyi mtshon cha dpal zhi bar byed do//grog mkhar gyi 'jim pa las byas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i rkang pa'i 
2b4(rgyud): mthil du sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bcug ste/bzhi mdor sbas na 'gro ba dang 'ong ba thams cad rengs par byed cing rtag par byed pa thams cad rengs par byed/gro ba'am gos la sngags kyi rgyal po 'di bris la spra tshil gyis 
2b5(rgyud): btums te de nyid kyi khong par bcug la/bum pa ser ba chus bkang ba'i nang du bcug na yongs su brtag pa rengs par byed do//bla gos kyi mtha' ma la sngags rjes su dran zhing mdud pa bcings pas lam du rkun po la sogs pa 
2b6(rgyud): rengs par byed cing /nags kyi ba men la sogs pa rwa can dang mche ba can rnams rengs par byed do//pong ba la lan bdun bsngags te chur bcug na/chu na rgyu ba'i kha 'ching bar byed do//dur khrod kyi ras la pha rol gyi dpung 
2b7(rgyud): gi tshogs kyi skyes bu'i gtso bo'i ming smos pa'i sngags sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su bris la/sna tshogs rdo rje'i phyi rol du yi ge lam brgyad bris shing phyi rol dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor dang ldan par bris te/phyogs rdo rjes brgyan 
2b8(rgyud): pa'i spra tshil las byas pa'i tshogs kyi bdag po'i khong par bcug pa'i sngags/pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs kyis mtshams kyi bar du bcug na dpung gi tshogs rengs par byed do//sngags oM gnyis kyi kha sbyor du bcug la yi ge 
3a1(rgyud): @#//Tha zung gis yongs su bskor ba/phyi rol tu yi ge lam brgyad kyis bskor ba dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyis gnon pa/sna tshogs rdo rjes nye bar mdzes par byas pa/grog mkhar gyi 'jim pa las byas pa'i 
3a2(rgyud): tshogs kyi bdag po'i khong par bcug la dur khrod kyi bum pa'i nang du bcug nas dur khrod du sbas pa'am/phub ma'i sbyin bsreg gis pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs rengs par byed do//grong khyer tshig pa'i me la mngon du phyogs shing 
3a3(rgyud): snga/sa 'dis mngon par bsngags pa'i chu snyim pa bdun rab tu gtor na khyim gang bsrung bar 'dod pa'i khyim de srung bar byed do//thal bas mgul pa bcings pas skyugs pa zlog par byed do//mthe'u chung gis lce dam du 
3a4(rgyud): btsir la sngags brjod pas skyigs bu med par byed do//thal ba la mngon par sngags pas mig rengs pa sos par byed do//'brum bu la sogs pa rnam par 'tshe ba la ras kyi dum bu la bris te/khyim gang gi sgor btags pa'i 
3a5(rgyud): khyim der 'brum bu la sogs pa rnams par 'tshe ba zlog par byed do//nya dang sha dang chang dang 'bras chan la sogs pa sbyin par 'gyur pa las grong gi phyi rol gyi char gtor ma chen po btang bar bya ba dang yams shing lnga dang 'bru 
3a6(rgyud): lnga'i bye brag mar la gtags pas grong gi dbus su zhi ba'i thab khung byas la sbyin sreg byas na bdud dang rnam par 'tshe ba thams cad rengs par byed do//gang 'ga' zhig la grong la sogs pa sbyin par 'gyu ba la bzlas 
3a7(rgyud): pa tsam gyis zlog par byed do//rtag tu bzlas na lha dang lha ma yin dang lto 'phye chen po rnams kyis mi mthong bar 'gyur ro//dpal rje btsun sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mngon par byang chub pa'i dus su//bdud bzhi 
3a8(rgyud): las rgyal bar bya ba'i don du sngags kyi rgyal po 'di mdzod spu las rnam par phyung ba'i phyir mdzod spu'i nor bu zhes bya bar grags pa yin no//gseg ma la mngon par bsngags te/gtor ba sbrul dag 'byer bar byed do//
3b1(rgyud): khyim gyi rtsig ngos la zer la rdo rgyus la mngon par bsngags pas bris na sbrul gyis zil gyis non par mi 'gyur ro//yang na rdo leb la sngags gzod bus brkos te bris pa sbas na ri dgas dang sbrul gdug pa rnams khyim 'am grong ngam grong khyer 
3b2(rgyud): du sbas na ci srid 'dod pa'i bar du mche ba 'ching bar byed do//phyung bas grol bar 'gyur ro//sngags 'di ma bsgrubs su zin kyang las thams cad byed par 'gyur ro//gseg ma la sogs pa la yongs su bzlas te gtor na mche ba can thams 
3b3(rgyud): cad kyi mche ba 'ching bar byed de//dbang chen gyi dkyil 'khor gyis khyab pa'i sna tshogs rdo rje'i dbus su bsgrub pa bya ba'i ming dang bcas pa'i sngags thams cad bri bar bya'o//dur khrod kyi ro sregs pa'i shing gi phur bu la lan bdun mngon par bsngags

3b6(rgyud): par the tshom med do//lan tshwa dang ske tshe mnyam pa'i sbyin sreg gam phub ma'i sbyin sreg gis pha rol gyi dpung gi tshogs rnams 'byer bar byed do//oM am+sha sim+sha kA s+ya su dzo ma phe kha e du s+ya su swAhA/zhes byung ngo //oM a bA shi ma b+hA ka bA su dze ma phi du pA su/sngags kyi 
3b7(rgyud): rgyal po 'di ma sgrubs su zin kyang rtag tu bzlas na 'jigs pa thams cad zlog par byed do//rdo rje'i chu'i thig les dgra bo thams cad rmongs par byed cing khros pas rab tu dang bar byed do//bcings pa la bzlas na bcings pa grol bar byed do//phongs par gyur pas bzlas na rnyed pa

45a5(rgyud): bzad dang /mi yi sdug bsngal mang po gang yin pa//gser gyi rnga sgra mthu stobs dbang gis ni//nyon mongs sgrib pa thams cad gtan zhi nas//dper na thub dbang bcom ldan ji bzhin du//'jigs pa bsal nas bde ba nyid du 
45a6(rgyud): shog/bcom ldan 'khor ba'i rgya mtsho chen por ni//spyod bsags thams cad mkhyen ni bsgrubs pas na//sems can byang chub yan lag ldan mdzad nas//mthar ni yon tan rgya mtsho phyogs par mdzad//gser 'od dam pa'i rnga 
45a7(rgyud): chen sgra 'di ni//thos pa kun ni tshangs dbyangs 'thob par shog/bla med byang chub 'bras bu thob gyur nas//chos kyi 'khor lo gya nom rtag bskor shog/tshe ni bskal pa bsam mi khyab gnas nas//ji bzhin chos bshad skye dgu 
45a8(rgyud): phan byed shog/nyon mongs sdug bsngal mang po rgyun bcad nas//'dod sdang gti mug la sogs zhi bar shog/sems can gang zhig ngan 'gro gnas gyur nas//me chen rab 'bar lus kun khyab pa dag/rnga sgra dam pa 'di ni 
45b1(rgyud): thos gyur na//sdug bsngal bral nas sangs rgyas skyabs 'gro shog/thams cad mngon gnas shes pa grub gyur nas//'das pa'i tshe rabs brgya stong dran par shog/thub pa'i gtso bo yang dag dran gyur nas//de bzhin 
45b2(rgyud): gshegs bstan zab mo thos par shog/gser gyi rnga dbyangs dam pa thos pa'i dbang //rtag par sangs rgyas rnams ni nyer bsten nas//thams cad sdig pa'i las ni yongs spangs te//rnam dag dge phyogs 'ba' zhig 
45b3(rgyud): spyod par shog/lha dang mi yi srog chags thams cad kun//gus pas smon lam rtse gcig 'debs pa dag/gser gyi rnga sgra dam pa thos gyur nas//bsam pa rnams ni yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur//sems can gang zhig mnar 
45b4(rgyud): med lhung gyur nas//dmyal ba'i 'bar mas lus ni nyon mongs te//mgon skyabs med cing 'khor bar gnas gyur pa//thos par gyur na sdug bsngal zhi bar 'gyur//lha mi yi dgas dud 'gro'i skye gnas su//sdug bsngal 
45b5(rgyud): bgegs mang mngon du myong 'gyur ba//gser gyi rnga dbyangs dmapa thos gyur na//sdug bsngal spangs nas thar pa thob par 'gyur//da ltar phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten na//rtag tu bzhugs pa rkang gnyis gtso//thugs rje chen po'i 
45b6(rgyud): thugs kyis ni//brtse bas bdag la dgongs su gsol//sems can gang zhig skyabs ma mchis//mgon ni bgyid pa ma mchis pa//de lta bu yi rnam pa la//skyabs chen dag ni bgyid par shog/bdag gis sngon ni bgyis 
45b7(rgyud): pa'i sdig/las ngan shin tu lci ba rnams//deng bdag stobs bcu'i spyan sngar ni//thams cad rtse gcig rab tu bshags//bdag gis sangs rgyas yid ma ches//bla ma gnyen shes ma gus dang //dge ba mang po ma spyad pas//
45b8(rgyud): rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa'am//bdag gis mtho btsun rus rigs dang //longs spyod dag ni snyems pa dang //dar la bab pas dregs gyur nas//rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa 'am//rtag tu log pa'i bsam bskyed nas//
46a1(rgyud): ngag gis tshig ngan smras pa dang //kha na ma tho ma mthong nas//rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa 'am//rtag tu byis pa'i blo spyad nas//ma rig mun pas sems g.yogs dang //mi dge'i grogs po'i rjes 'brangs nas//
46a2(rgyud): rtag tu las ngan bgyis pa'am//rtsed mo dga' ba rnams kyi rgyus//mya ngan dag dang ldan 'gyur zhing //'dod chags zhe sdang dkris pas na//de slad sdig rnams bgyis pa'am//mi dge grogs po bsten pa dang //phrag dog ser 
46a3(rgyud): sna bsam pa dang //dbul phongs gya gyu spyad pas na//de slad sdig ma rnams bgyis pa'am//sdig mang dag la mi mos kyang //'jigs skrag dag dang bcas pa'am//rang dbang dag ni ma mchis pas//de slad sdig rnams gyis pa 
46a4(rgyud): 'am//rnam par g.yeng ba'i bsam pa dang //khong khro khon du 'dzin pa dang //bkres skom dag gis gdungs pa'i rgyus//de slad sdig rnams kyis pa'am//bza' dang btung dang gos dag dang //bud med dag la chags sred pa'i//
46a5(rgyud): nyon mongs mes ni bsregs pas na//de slad sdigs rnams bgyis pa'am//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun la//gus pa'i sems ni ma skyed pa'i//de 'dra'i sdig mang gang bgyis pa//deng bdag thams cad so sor bshags//
46a6(rgyud): rang rgyal byang chub sems dpa' la//gus pa'i sems nim mchis pa'i//de 'dra'i sdig med gang bgyis pa//deng bad thams cad so sor bshag//mi shes dam chos skur ba dang //pha ma sri zhum bgyis pa'i//de 'dra'i 
46a7(rgyud): sdig med gang bgyis pa/deng bdag thams cad so so bshags//gti mug dang ni de rgyal dang //'dod chags zhe sdang mthu yis ni//de 'dra'i sdig med gong bgyis pa//deng bdag la thams cad so so bshag//bdag 
46a8(rgyud): gis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams//sangs rgyas grangs med gang mchod pa//sems can rnams ni rab drangs nas//sdug bsngal bgegs rnams bral bgyid shog/sems can dag ni thams cad kun//
46b1(rgyud): sa bcu la ni gnas gyur te//bsod nams ye shes yongs rdzogs nas//mngon sangs skye dgu 'dren par shog/bdag gis sems can kun gyi slad//bskal pa brgya stong dka' spyad nas//shes rab chen po'i stobs kyis ni//
46b2(rgyud): sdug bsngal rgya mtsho mthar bgyid shog/bdag gis srog chags kun gyi slad//gser 'od mchog tu rnam par rgyal//zab mo'i mdo sde bshad nas ni//las ngan rnams ni bsal bar shog/gang zhig bskal pa brgya stong 
46b3(rgyud): du//sdig lci dag ni bgyis pa rnams//skad cig tsam zhig 'thol gshags na//sdig pa mang po ma lus 'byung //gser 'od dam pa 'di bzhin du//de ltar bshags pa bgyis gyur na//sdug bsngal las mang thams cad kun//
46b4(rgyud): 'di yi dbang gis myur du 'byang //ting 'dzin mchog ni brgya stong dang //bsam gyis mi khyab gzungs rnams dang //dbang stobs byang chub lam yan lag/rtag tu sgom yang skye mi 'gyur//bdag ni rin chen phun sum tshogs//
46b5(rgyud): sa bcu la ni phyin gyur nas//sangs rgyas yon tan yongs rdzogs te//'khor bar 'bab pa sgrol bar shog/bdag gis sangs rgyas mtsho las ni//shin tu zab mo yon tan mdzod//gya nom ye shes bsam dka' ba//
46b6(rgyud): phun sum tshogs pa thob par shog/phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kun//bdag la so sor gzigs dgongs nas//thugs rje chen po'i thugs kyis ni//bdag gis bshags pa bzhes su gsol//bdag gis 
46b7(rgyud): bskal pa mang por ni las ngan mang po bgyis pas na//de yi dbang gis sdug bsngal myong //thugs rjes byang bar mdzad par smon//bdag gis las ngan mang bgyis pa//rtag tu mya ngan 'jigs sems skyes//
46b8(rgyud): spyod lam bzhi po kun tu yang //dga' ba'i 'du shes yod ma mchis//sangs rgyas thugs rjes chen po ldan//sems can 'jigs pa sel mdzad na//bdag gis bshags pa pa bzhes nas ni//mya ngan sdug bsngal bral 
47a1(rgyud): par smon//bdag la nyon mongs sgrib pa dang //rnam smin las ni ci mchis pa//thugs rje chen po'i chib kyis ni//rab bkrus byang bar mdzad par smon//bdag gis sngon chad sdig pa dang //da ltar las ngan bgyis 
47a2(rgyud): pa rnams//rtse cig sems kyis 'thol bshags na//thams cad rab tu byang bar shog/ma 'ongs pa'i las ngan rnams//bsdams te slong bar yod mi bgyid//gal te 'gal ba mchis na yang //nams kyang 'chab par yongs mi 
47a3(rgyud): bgyid//lus gsum dag gi rnam pa bzhi//yid kyi las ni rnam pa gsum//sems can rnams ni 'ching bgyid pas//thog ma med nas rgyun mi 'chad//spyod pa 'di gsum dbang gis ni//mi dge las bcu bgyis gyur pas//
47a4(rgyud): de 'dra'i nongs pa mang po rnams//de dag thams cad so sor bshags//bdag gis las ngan mang bgyis pas//sdug bsngal rnam smin rang nyid myong //sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyan sngar ni//bsam pa thag pas so sor bshags//
47a5(rgyud): 'dzam bu'i gling ni 'di dag dang //'jig rten khams ni gzhan dag tu//dge ba'i las mang ci mchis pa//deng bdag thams cad yid rang ngo //mi dge las bcu rab spangs nas//dge bcu lam ni mngon bsgrubs te//sa bcu dag la 
47a6(rgyud): rab gnas nas//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas rtag mthong smon//bdag gis lus dang ngag yid kyis//bsod nams ye shes las bsgrubs pa'i//dge ba'i rtsa ba 'di'i mthus//bla med ye shes myur grub shog/deng bdag 
47a7(rgyud): stobs bcu'i spyan sngar mngon sum du//sdug bsngal bgegs med sna tshogs mthol zhing bshags//byis pa srid gsum rmongs pa'i bgegs dag dang //rtag par las ngan lci bar bgyis pa'i bgegs//bdag gi log pa'i 
47a8(rgyud): 'dod pa bsags pa'i bgegs//rtag tu sred pa skyed 'khor bar 'gyur ba'i bgegs//'jig rten 'di la mngon zhen chags pa'i bgegs//byis pa thams cad kun gyi nyon mongs bgegs//sems myos rnam g.yeng 
47b1(rgyud): phyin ci log pa'i bgegs/mi dge grogs po dag la bsten pa'i bgegs//'khor ba dag la 'dod chags zhen pa'i bgegs//zhe sdang gti mug glen pas sdig bgyis bgegs//mi khom brgyad skye mi dge'i grogs po'i bgegs//
47b2(rgyud): bsod nams yon tan ma bsags pa yi bgegs//deng bdag rgyal ba kun gyi spyan sngar ni//sdig pa'i las ngan mtha' las bgyis pa bshags//deng bdag bde gshegs kun la skyabs su mchi//bla med yon tan mtsho la 
47b3(rgyud): bdag phyag 'tshal//gser gyi ri ltar phyogs bcu gsal mdzad pa//byams dang thugs rjes bdag ni gzung du gsol//sku ni gser mdog rnam dag dri mi mnga'//spyan ni rnam dag bai dUr+ya dang 'dra//dpal dang gzi brjid grags 
47b4(rgyud): dang ldan pa'i gtso//thugs rje shes rab nyi mas mun sel ba//sangs rgyas nyi ma'i 'od kyis kun gsal mdzad//shin du rnam dag dri med rdul dang bral//thub pa'i zla bas gsal mdzad rab bsil ba//sems can nyon mongs 
47b5(rgyud): gdung ba sel bar mdzad//sum cu rtsa gnyis mtshan gyis rab brgyan cing //dpe byad bzang po brgyad bcu yongs su rdzogs//yon tan bsam dka' mtshungs pa ma mchis pas//nyi 'od lta bur 'jig rten//sal bar 
47b6(rgyud): mdzad//kha dog bai dUr+ya 'dra dri mi mnga'//zla ba nya ltar nam mkha' dag la bzhugs//shel mchog dam pa'i dra bas gser sku sgribs//'od zer sna tshogs dag gis rab tu brgyan//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal chu bo'i nang 
47b7(rgyud): du ni//rga nad mya ngan chu yis deng bas na//de 'dra'i sdug bsngal rgya mtsho bzod dka' ba//sangs rgyas nyi ma'i 'dod kyis gtan du bskams//deng bdag stong gsum 'jig rten rmad byung gtso//thams cad mkhyen la spyi btud phyag 
47b8(rgyud): 'tshal lo//'od zer rab gsal gser mdog 'dra ba'i sku//dpe byad dam pa sna tshogs dag gis brgyan//ji ltar rgya mtsho'i chu tshad shes dka' zhing //sa chen rdul ni bgrang du ma mchis dang //ji ltar ri rab srang grangs gzhal dka' 
48a1(rgyud): @#//zhing /nam mkha' dag la mu mtha' ma mchis ltar//sangs rgyas rnams kyi yon tan de bzhin te//sems can thams cad kun gyis rig mi 'gyur//bskal pa tshad med snyed du rab bsams kyang //yon tan rgya mtsho'i 
48a2(rgyud): pha mtha' rtogs mi 'gyur//sa chen 'di yi ri brag ji snyed pa//rdul phran bzhin du gses kyang brtsir rung zhing //rgya mtsho'i chab ni sbu rtses gzhal rung yang //sangs rgyas yon tan bgrang bar rdo mi thog/sems can thams cad 
48a3(rgyud): lhan cig 'dus nas ni//bcom ldan grags pa'i chos ni thams cad dang //mtshan bzangs rnam dag dkon mchog bsngags brjod kyang //de yi pha mtha' dpag par yongs mi 'gyur//bdag gi dge ba'i las mang ci mchis pas 
48a4(rgyud): myur du bla na ma mchis gtso grub nas//yang dag chos bshad skye dgu phan btags te//thams cad sdug bsngal mang las thar bgyid shog/bdud kyi dmag tshogs stobs kyang rab btul nas//bla med dam chos 'khor lo 
48a5(rgyud): bskor bar shog/skal pa grangs med bsam mi khyab gnas nas//bdud rtsi'i ro yis sems can tshim bgyid shog/'das pa'i rgyal ba rnams ni ji lta bzhin//pha rol phyin drug yongs su rdzogs bgyid shog/'dod 
48a6(rgyud): chags zhe sdang gti mug zhi gyur nas//nyon mongs btul te sdug bsngal bsal bar shog/bdag gi rtag tu tshe rabs dran gyur nas//'das pa'i skye ba brgya stong dran pa dang //rtag par thub pa'i gtso bo dran gyur te//sangs 
48a7(rgyud): rgyas zab mo'i chos ni thos par shog/bdag gi dge ba'i las ni 'di rnams kyis//rgyal ba tshad med dag la bsnyed bkur nas//mi dge ba yi rgyu kun yongs spangs te//yang dag dam chos rtag tu sgrub par shog/'jig 
48a8(rgyud): rten thams cad sems can ci snyed pa//sdug bsngal spangs nas bde ba thob par shog/dbang po ma tshang ji snyed mchis pa dag/de dag lus kyi mtshan rnams yongs rdzogs shog/sems can gang zhig nad kyi sdug 
48b1(rgyud): bsngal gyis//lus ni nyam chung skyabs bgyid ma mchis pa//thams cad nad kyi sdug bsngal rab zhi nas//dbang po kha dog mthu ni rgyas par shog/gang gis rgyal chos 'gal bgyis bsang la thug/sdug bsngal mang 
48b2(rgyud): gtses mya ngan rab skyes nas//de yis sdug bsngal mi bzad 'di dag myong ba'i tshe//mgon skyabs dag ni bgyi ba ma mchis dang //gang dag gzhu bdeg mtshon bcas bcings pa dang //gnod byed yo byad sna tshogs 
48b3(rgyud): lus nyon mongs//mya ngan brgya stong tshad med myong ba na//lus sems rab gzir bde ba cung zad med//de dag thams cad bcings shing brdegs pa dang //sdug bsngal nyon mongs dag las thar bar shog/bsad la 
48b4(rgyud): thug pa dag ni srog thob nas//sdug bsngal mang po thams cad gtan zad shog/sems can gang zhig bkres skom gdungs gyur pa//ro mchog dam pa sna tshogs 'theb par shog/long bas gzugs mthong 'on pas 
48b5(rgyud): sgra thos dang //thed pos 'gro nus lkugs pa smra nus shog/dbul phongs sems la rin chen gter thob nas//bang mdzod rab rgyas rgud pa ma mchis te//thams cad bde mchog dam pa rab rnyed nas//sems can sdug 
48b6(rgyud): bsngal myong ba ma mchis shog/lha mi thams cad mthong bar mos pa yi//byang bzhin dam pa rab mdzes thob gyur nas//bde ba tshad med mngon sum myong gyur te//longs spyod 'byor rgyas bsod nams ldan par shog/
48b7(rgyud): sems can de dag rol mo bsams pa bzhin//sgra mchog mang po mngon du 'byung bar shog/chu 'dod gser gyi me tog steng 'phyo ba//rab bsil rdzing bu mngon du snang bar shog/sems can de dag rol mo gsams pa 
48b8(rgyud): bzhin//sgra mchog mang po 'don du 'byung rnams dang //gser dngul dang ni rin chen bai dUr+ya//rgyan phreng thams cad phun sum tshogs par shog/sems can mi snyan thos par mi 'gyur zhing //phan tshun mi mthun mthong bar

50a1(rgyud): @#//gyis brlabs pa'i mthus rgyu rkyen 'di dag khyed la bstan pa yin par rig par bya'o//de'i tshe 'khor mang po rnams de skad ces bka' stsal pa thos nas thams cad rab tu dga' zhing mngon par yid ches nas 
50a2(rgyud): bka' bzhin du spyod par gyur to//gser 'od dam pa mchog tu rnam par rgyal ba mdo sde'i rgyal po las rmi lam na 'gyod tshangs byed pa mthong ba'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////gser 'od dam pa mchog du rnam par rgyal ba

50b1(rgyud): bskor ba byas nas phyogs cig tu 'khod do//de nas brgya byin lha'i dbang po bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyi rlabs kyi mthus stan las langs te bla gos phrag pa cig tu gzang nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga 
50b2(rgyud): la ba der logs su thal mo sbyar nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rigs kyi bu 'am/rigs kyi bu mo gang gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub gnyer zhing theg pa chen po mngon par bsgrubs

50b4(rgyud): kyis brgya byin lha'i dbang po la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu deng khyed kyis tshad med dpag tu med pa'i sems can rnams rnal 'byor bsgoms nas rnam par dag pa'i rnam par grol ba'i bde ba 'thob par bya ba dang /'jig rten thams cad

282b4(rgyud): rgyal ba dran par bgyid//rgyal ba mthong bar yang ni rtag tu mya ngan bgyid//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyi ma mthong bar bgyi slad du//rtag par yang ni smon lam rab tu 'debs par bgyid//bdag ni rtag tu dog sar 
282b5(rgyud): pus mo btsugs nas su//rgyal ba'i gzugs kyis skom pas rab tu mya ngan sems//'dren pa'i slad du snying rje'i skad kyis du ba bgyid//bdag ni bde bar gshegs pa mthong ba rab tu skom//mya ngan me yis 
282b6(rgyud): kun tu rab tu 'bar bar 'gyur//mthong ba'i chu bsil bdag la rtag par stsal du gsol//bdag ni khyod kyi gzugs la lta bas rab tu skom//thugs rje'i chab kyis bdag la rab tu tshim par mdzod//'dren pa khyod ni 
282b7(rgyud): bdag la thugs rje mdzad du gsol//zhi ba'i gzugs mthong ba ni bdag la stsal du gsol//khyod ni lha dang bcas pa'i 'gro ba'i skyob pa ste//'di ltar nyan thos rnams kyi lus ni stong pa lags//sems can thams cad 
282b8(rgyud): dag ni rmi lam rang bzhin te//nam mkha' 'dra zhing nam mkha'i ngo bo nyid kyang lags//sgyu ma smig rgyu dang ni chu yi zla ba dang //'dren pa khyod kyi stong pa che dang ldan pa lags//de nas bcom ldan 'das gdan las bzhengs te/tshangs 
283a1(rgyud): pa'i sgra skad kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi lha mo khyod legs so//khyod yang legs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas rigs kyi lha mo byang chub yang dag par bsdus pa dang /lha mo chen mo dbyangs can la sogs 
283a2(rgyud): pa lha 'a bu mo de dag thams cad dang //lha mo chen mo dpal la gsogs pa lha'i tshogs de dag thams cad dang rnam thos kyi bu la sogs pa lha'i rgyal po de dag thams cad dang thams cad dang ldan pa'i 
283a3(rgyud): 'khor de dang //lha dang mi dang //lha ma yin dang dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do////gser 'od dam pa mdo sde'i dbang 
283a4(rgyud): po'i rgyal po las/'jug sdud pa'i le'u zhes bya ste nyi shu dgu pa'o////'phags pa gser 'od dam pa mdo sde dbang po'i rgyal po zhes 
283a5(rgyud): bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs s+ho//pan Di ta dzi na mi tra dang //shi len dra bo d+hi dang //zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba ban d+he ye shes sdes bsgyur cing zhus te//skad 
283a6(rgyud): gsar chad kyis gtan la phab pa//ye d+ha rmA he tu pra b+ha bA he tun te ShAn ta thA ga to h+ya ba data//te ShAny+tsa yo ni ro d+ha e bam+bA dI mahA shra ma NaHye swAhA//
